#### [2. Awesome Web Performance Budget](/content/pajaydev/awesome-web-performance-budget/README.md)

##### Learn / Articles

* [Performance budgets 101](https://web.dev/articles/performance-budgets-101) - What a budget is and how to start setting one.
* [Your first performance budget](https://web.dev/articles/your-first-performance-budget) - Define your first performance budget in a few simple steps.
* [Incorporate performance budgets into your build process](https://web.dev/articles/incorporate-performance-budgets-into-your-build-tools) - Enforce budgets with webpack, bundlesize and Lighthouse.
* [Start Performance Budgeting](https://addyosmani.com/blog/performance-budgets/) - Addy Osmani's guide to choosing metrics and setting budgets.
* [Can You Afford It?: Real-world Web Performance Budgets](https://infrequently.org/2017/10/can-you-afford-it-real-world-web-performance-budgets/) - Alex Russell on deriving budgets from real devices and networks.
* [How to Make a Performance Budget](https://web.archive.org/web/20230224133739/http://v3.danielmall.com/articles/how-to-make-a-performance-budget/) - Dan Mall's step-by-step approach to building a budget (archived).
* [Setting a Web Performance Budget](https://web.archive.org/web/20241110185738/https://www.invisionapp.com/inside-design/setting-a-web-performance-budget/) - InVision's Inside Design guide to setting a budget (archived).
* [Web Performance Budget: How to Set Up, Calculate, and Apply](https://uxify.com/blog/web-performance-budget-guide/) - End-to-end guide from choosing metrics to applying the budget.
* [Performance as Design](https://bradfrost.com/blog/post/performance-as-design/) - Brad Frost on treating performance as an essential design feature.
* [Front-end Performance for Web Designers and Front-end Developers](https://csswizardry.com/2013/01/front-end-performance-for-web-designers-and-front-end-developers/) - Harry Roberts' primer on front-end performance fundamentals.
* [How Angular CLI Budgets Saved My Day](https://medium.com/dailyjs/how-did-angular-cli-budgets-save-my-day-and-how-they-can-save-yours-300d534aae7a) - Implementing performance budgets in Angular projects.

##### Learn / Videos

* [Implementing Performance Budgets](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=vVlpCmK1l5k) - #AskChrome Live session on using budgets to prevent regressions.
* [Design Decisions Through the Lens of a Performance Budget](https://vimeo.com/108328247) - Making smarter design decisions from the start of a project so the site performs well.

##### Learn / Books

* [Designing for Performance](https://designingforperformance.com/) - Lara Hogan's book on weighing aesthetics and speed, free to read online.

##### Learn / Case Studies

* [WPO Stats](https://wpostats.com/) - Case studies and experiments showing the impact of web performance on user experience and business metrics.
* [Netflix: Shipping Less JavaScript](https://medium.com/dev-channel/a-netflix-web-performance-case-study-c0bcde26a9d9) - A Netflix web performance case study.
* [Pinterest PWA](https://medium.com/dev-channel/a-pinterest-progressive-web-app-performance-case-study-3bd6ed2e6154) - A Pinterest Progressive Web App performance case study.
* [Tinder PWA](https://medium.com/@addyosmani/a-tinder-progressive-web-app-performance-case-study-78919d98ece0) - A Tinder Progressive Web App performance case study.
* [Twitter Lite](https://medium.com/@paularmstrong/twitter-lite-and-high-performance-react-progressive-web-apps-at-scale-d28a00e780a3) - Building a high-performance React PWA at scale.
* [Treebo PWA](https://medium.com/dev-channel/treebo-a-react-and-preact-progressive-web-app-performance-case-study-5e4f450d5299) - A React and Preact PWA performance case study.
* [The Telegraph: Creating a Web Performance Culture](https://medium.com/the-telegraph-engineering/improving-third-party-web-performance-at-the-telegraph-a0a1000be5) - Improving third-party performance at The Telegraph.
* [Casper.com: Self-hosting Optimizely](https://medium.com/caspertechteam/we-shaved-1-7-seconds-off-casper-com-by-self-hosting-optimizely-2704bcbff8ec) - How Casper shaved 1.7 seconds off load time.
* [Smashing Magazine](https://www.smashingmagazine.com/2014/09/improving-smashing-magazine-performance-case-study/) - Improving Smashing Magazine's web performance.
* [BBC: Cutting the Mustard](https://responsivenews.co.uk/post/18948466399/cutting-the-mustard) - How BBC News served a lean core experience to every browser.

##### Learn / Blogs

* [Web Performance Calendar](https://calendar.perfplanet.com/) - Yearly advent calendar of web performance articles, a favorite of speed geeks.

##### Learn / Podcasts

* [Chasing Waterfalls](https://web.archive.org/web/20241208004024/https://chasingwaterfalls.io/) - Conversations with people making the web faster, hosted by [Tim Kadlec](https://timkadlec.com/) (archived).
* [ShopTalk Show](https://shoptalkshow.com/) - A podcast about building websites that often covers performance.

##### Finance

##### Fun Tools

* [ArtiGram](https://artigram.cafeboon.com) - A free gallery where people and AI assistants share interactive things made with AI, such as small apps, games and calculators. `#free`

##### Gaming

* [Ludo.ai](https://ludo.ai/) - Generates game assets from a prompt or reference image: sprites, spritesheet animations, 3D models, sound effects and music. `#freemium`

##### Image Generator

* [TattooTryOn](https://tattootryon.app/) - Placing and sizing a tattoo on your photo is free in the browser. When it looks right, 5 credits buys a realistic render. Plans from $4.99 a month. `#freemium` `#design`

##### Music

* [BeatMusic](https://beatmusic.ai/) - AI music generator that turns a text idea or your lyrics into full songs with vocals. `#freemium`

##### Productivity

* [Alter Ego](https://cloudalterego.cafeboon.com) - One memory for all your AI chats, in Claude, ChatGPT, Gemini and any other AI chat, logged automatically and kept in your own cloud storage. `#mcp` `#memory`
* [Launch OS](https://launchautomation.ai/launch-os) - Combines business contacts, sales pipelines, tasks and reports with an AI assistant grounded in workspace records. `#paid`
* [Pexi](https://chromewebstore.google.com/detail/hmilmocmhfcnkaodecjpknjgfeegepoo) - Chrome extension for comparing shared tabs with source links, filling forms, and drafting emails with your own AI API key (paid after a 7-day card-backed trial). `#paid`
* [QuelLLM](https://quelllm.fr/) - Browser-based calculator and configurator for comparing local AI models against available hardware; results are estimates and the tool does not infer responses. `#free` `#productivity`

* [GuruTrans](https://gurutrans.app/) - AI translation tools for languages, old English styles, and Morse code. Registered users get 5 free translations a day. `#freemium` `#translation`

##### Travel

* [CheapCarRent](https://cheapcarent.com/) - Official hosted Streamable HTTP MCP server for car rental search at 15,000 pickup points in 46 countries, 19 languages. No API key, read-only. `#free` `#mcp` `#opensource`

##### Video

* [BeatMV](https://beatmv.ai/) - AI music video generator that turns any song into a music video that moves with the beat. `#freemium`
* [Cuecaps](https://cuecaps.com/) - Animated word-by-word captions for Shorts, TikTok and Reels, transcribed and rendered in the browser so the video never uploads; exports MP4, transparent overlays and SRT/VTT. `#freemium`
* [PawMambo](https://pawmambo.com/) - Creates short AI dog dance videos from a dog photo and a dance-reference video. `#freemium` `#video` `#pets`

#### [8. Awesome Analytics](/content/newTendermint/awesome-analytics/README.md)

##### General analytics

* [Eventkiwi](https://eventkiwi.com/) - Event aggregation and reporting platform: send events from any service through one HTTP API and get scheduled reports, with an AI summary built on exact counts, by email or Telegram. `©` `SaaS`
* [North Metric](https://www.northmetric.io/) - Churn analytics for SaaS on Stripe. Scores each customer's churn risk and ranks them by revenue at risk. `©` `SaaS`

##### SEO

* [Firecrawl Alexandria](https://alexandria.firecrawl.dev/categories/web-analytics-marketing) - Website traffic and engagement (Similarweb) and Wayback Machine snapshots as tools AI agents can call through one Firecrawl API key, CLI or MCP server. `©` `SaaS`

##### Privacy focused analytics

* [Owlytics](https://kyrylo.org/owlytics) - Small, cookieless web analytics with one Rust executable and one SQLite database. No stored raw IP addresses. ([Source Code (⭐5)](https://github.com/kyrylo/owlytics) ⭐ 5 | 🐛 0 | 🌐 Rust | 📅 2026-11-18) `MIT` `Self-Hosted` `Rust`
* [Highseat](https://highseat.com/) - Cookieless web analytics with revenue by source: connects to your payment provider and shows which traffic sources bring paying customers. Public API and MCP server. `©` `SaaS`

##### Analytics layers

* [hypequery](https://hypequery.com) - Open-source embedded analytics layer for ClickHouse. Define metrics and dimensions once in TypeScript or Python, then serve them as type-safe queries, authenticated HTTP APIs, React hooks and MCP tools for AI agents from your own backend. ([Source Code (⭐106)](https://github.com/hypequery/hypequery) ⭐ 106 | 🐛 15 | 🌐 TypeScript | 📅 2026-11-22) `Apache-2.0` `TypeScript/Python`

##### Attribution tracking

* [Improvado](https://improvado.io/) - Marketing analytics platform that combines ad, web analytics and CRM data for cross-channel reporting and attribution, with an MCP server for AI agents. `©` `SaaS`

##### Social media analytics

* [Social Fetch](https://www.socialfetch.dev) - Social media data API: public profiles, posts, comments and transcripts from TikTok, Instagram, YouTube, X, Reddit and LinkedIn as JSON. `©` `SaaS`

##### Developer analytics

* [Aidealy](https://aidealy.ai) - Measures the productivity of engineers and AI agents from the code they ship: a rank per engineer, how much of the code AI wrote, and AI token cost per pull request. `©` `SaaS`
* [Weave](https://weaveos.com/) - Engineering analytics for code output, review quality, and AI-tool usage and costs. `©` `SaaS`

##### Analytics dashboards

* [Web-to-Web BI Board](https://www.roimobi.com/open-source/cohort-roas-ltv-dashboard) - Cohort ROAS and LTV dashboard for subscription web funnels: payback from day 0 to day 360, sBG forecasts with 80 % intervals, real-time spend and a funnel map. Static, reads one JSON file, no backend. ([Demo](https://ua5er.github.io/web2web-bi-board/), [Source Code (⭐0)](https://github.com/ua5er/web2web-bi-board) ⭐ 0 | 🐛 0 | 🌐 JavaScript | 📅 2026-11-17) `MIT` `JavaScript`

#### [9. Awesome LaTeX](/content/egeerardyn/awesome-LaTeX/README.md)

##### Engines

* [SwiftLaTeX (⭐2.3k)](https://github.com/SwiftLaTeX/SwiftLaTeX) ⭐ 2,320 | 🐛 18 | 🌐 C | 📅 2024-06-18 - pdfTeX and XeTeX compiled to WebAssembly, so you can compile LaTeX entirely in the browser or embed a TeX engine in a webpage. ![foss](https://github.com/egeerardyn/awesome-LaTeX/raw/master/fig/foss.svg)
* [XeTeX](https://xetex.sourceforge.net) - TeX compiler that provides better unicode and font support than TeX/pdfTeX (i.e. you can use the fonts of your operating system instead of only TeX fonts). ![foss](https://github.com/egeerardyn/awesome-LaTeX/raw/master/fig/foss.svg)

##### LaTeX formulas on the web

* [MathJax](https://www.mathjax.org) - JavaScript engine to render mathematical formulas on the web. The outcome looks really slick. ![foss](https://github.com/egeerardyn/awesome-LaTeX/raw/master/fig/foss.svg)
* [KaTeX](https://katex.org) - Math rendering library made by Khan Academy focusing on fast load times. All output is processed as plain HTML instead of fixed images. ![foss](https://github.com/egeerardyn/awesome-LaTeX/raw/master/fig/foss.svg)
* [MathLive](https://mathlive.io/) - Web component for editing and displaying math with LaTeX, including virtual keyboards and MathML/speech export. ![foss](https://github.com/egeerardyn/awesome-LaTeX/raw/master/fig/foss.svg)

##### LaTeX-focused

* [Gummi (⭐802)](https://github.com/alexandervdm/gummi) ⭐ 802 | 🐛 93 | 🌐 C | 📅 2024-11-21 - Simple GTK LaTeX editor with a continuously updating live preview pane. ![linux](https://github.com/egeerardyn/awesome-LaTeX/raw/master/fig/linux.svg) ![foss](https://github.com/egeerardyn/awesome-LaTeX/raw/master/fig/foss.svg)
* [LaTeX Editor (⭐35)](https://github.com/s-balli/latex-editor) ⭐ 35 | 🐛 0 | 🌐 Python | 📅 2026-11-20 - Single-executable desktop editor with live PDF preview, two-way SyncTeX, LaTeX-aware spell checking, and one-key project snapshots written as a standard Git repository. Needs a separate TeX distribution. ![windows](https://github.com/egeerardyn/awesome-LaTeX/raw/master/fig/windows.svg) ![linux](https://github.com/egeerardyn/awesome-LaTeX/raw/master/fig/linux.svg) ![foss](https://github.com/egeerardyn/awesome-LaTeX/raw/master/fig/foss.svg)
* [BakomaTex](https://bakoma-tex.com) - Commercial LaTeX editor that allows to edit your document both using its source code and WYSIWYG.
* [Texpile](https://texpile.com) - Free and open-source desktop editor that edits LaTeX visually (like LyX) or by source with IntelliSense, with local compilation and built-in Git. Also supports Typst and Markdown. ![foss](https://github.com/egeerardyn/awesome-LaTeX/raw/master/fig/foss.svg)
* [ModernTex](https://purplelink.llc/moderntex/) - Native macOS LaTeX editor for academic writing, with synced source and PDF, plain-language error messages, submission-readiness checks, a visual TikZ designer and a table editor. Free 7-day trial, then a one-time $19.99. ![mac](https://github.com/egeerardyn/awesome-LaTeX/raw/master/fig/apple.svg)

##### Online editors

* [SciTeX Cloud (⭐2)](https://github.com/scitex-ai/scitex-hub) ⭐ 2 | 🐛 21 | 🌐 Python | 📅 2026-11-22 - Self-hostable online editor with AI assistant integration, figure/table/citation management, real-time collaboration, and an MCP server (29 tools). ![foss](https://github.com/egeerardyn/awesome-LaTeX/raw/master/fig/foss.svg)
* [TeXlyre](https://texlyre.github.io/texlyre/) - Local-first, open-source browser editor for LaTeX with in-browser WebAssembly compilation, offline editing, and peer-to-peer collaboration via WebRTC. ![foss](https://github.com/egeerardyn/awesome-LaTeX/raw/master/fig/foss.svg)
* [CoCalc](https://cocalc.ai/features/latex-editor) - Collaborative online LaTeX editor with real-time editing, TimeTravel history, and first-class SageTeX, PythonTeX, and Knitr support. Freemium.
* [VerbTeX](https://verbosus.com/) - LaTeX editor for the browser plus native Android, iOS, and Windows apps, with optional offline compilation on device. Freemium.
* [Murfy](https://www.murfy.ai/en) - Online LaTeX editor with real-time collaboration and built-in AI assistance for writing and error fixing. Freemium.
* [Aldine](https://aldine.dev) - Self-hosted online editor with real-time collaboration in which every project is a Git repository, with branches in the editor and push/pull to GitHub, GitLab and Gitea. ![foss](https://github.com/egeerardyn/awesome-LaTeX/raw/master/fig/foss.svg)

##### Bibliography tools

* [betterbib (⭐841)](https://github.com/texworld/betterbib) ⭐ 841 | 🐛 3 | 📅 2026-11-10 - Command-line utility for improving your BibTeX files. Fetches information from online sources. ![mac](https://github.com/egeerardyn/awesome-LaTeX/raw/master/fig/apple.svg) ![windows](https://github.com/egeerardyn/awesome-LaTeX/raw/master/fig/windows.svg) ![linux](https://github.com/egeerardyn/awesome-LaTeX/raw/master/fig/linux.svg) ![foss](https://github.com/egeerardyn/awesome-LaTeX/raw/master/fig/foss.svg)
* [CrossRef Local (⭐5)](https://github.com/scitex-ai/crossref-local) ⭐ 5 | 🐛 0 | 🌐 Python | 📅 2026-10-17 - Local CrossRef database (167M papers) with full-text search, impact factor data, Python API for bibliography enrichment, and an MCP server (15 tools). ![foss](https://github.com/egeerardyn/awesome-LaTeX/raw/master/fig/foss.svg)
* [OpenAlex Local (⭐4)](https://github.com/scitex-ai/openalex-local) ⭐ 4 | 🐛 0 | 🌐 Python | 📅 2026-10-17 - Local OpenAlex database (284M scholarly works) with abstracts and semantic search for literature discovery, and an MCP server. ![foss](https://github.com/egeerardyn/awesome-LaTeX/raw/master/fig/foss.svg)
* [BiblioAudit (⭐1)](https://github.com/Official-Satyam-Tiwari/Reference-Architect) ⭐ 1 | 🐛 0 | 🌐 Python | 📅 2026-10-20 - Command-line tool to check, verify, and repair citations in BibTeX files across 12+ academic databases. ![mac](https://github.com/egeerardyn/awesome-LaTeX/raw/master/fig/apple.svg) ![windows](https://github.com/egeerardyn/awesome-LaTeX/raw/master/fig/windows.svg) ![linux](https://github.com/egeerardyn/awesome-LaTeX/raw/master/fig/linux.svg) ![foss](https://github.com/egeerardyn/awesome-LaTeX/raw/master/fig/foss.svg)
* [Bibdesk](https://bibdesk.sourceforge.net) - Great bibliography editor for ![mac](https://github.com/egeerardyn/awesome-LaTeX/raw/master/fig/apple.svg).
* [Biber](https://ctan.org/pkg/biber) - Modern bibliography processor for biblatex, with Unicode support and more flexible sorting than BibTeX. ![foss](https://github.com/egeerardyn/awesome-LaTeX/raw/master/fig/foss.svg)
* [latexci BibTeX tools](https://latexci.com/tools/bibtex) - Browser-based .bib cleaner (removes duplicates, normalizes author names, escapes special characters) plus DOI, arXiv, PubMed and ISBN to BibTeX lookups, with no install and no account. ![foss](https://github.com/egeerardyn/awesome-LaTeX/raw/master/fig/foss.svg)

##### Build Tools

* [TeXLive.net](https://texlive.net/) - Public compile service (used by LearnLaTeX.org) that turns an HTTP POST of a LaTeX document into a PDF. ![foss](https://github.com/egeerardyn/awesome-LaTeX/raw/master/fig/foss.svg)
* [LaTeX.Online](https://latexonline.cc/) - Cloud compiler that builds a PDF from a Git repository, a URL, or pasted TeX; also available as a self-hosted Docker image. ![foss](https://github.com/egeerardyn/awesome-LaTeX/raw/master/fig/foss.svg)

##### Misc. Tools

* [SciTeX Writer (⭐4)](https://github.com/scitex-ai/scitex-writer) ⭐ 4 | 🐛 3 | 🌐 Python | 📅 2026-11-22 - Manuscript compilation system with templates for manuscripts, revisions, and supplementary materials, plus figure, table, and citation handling, and an MCP server (38 tools). ![foss](https://github.com/egeerardyn/awesome-LaTeX/raw/master/fig/foss.svg)
* [Kroki](https://kroki.io) - Unified HTTP API that renders text-based diagrams, including PGF/TikZ, to SVG, PNG, or PDF. Self-hostable. ![foss](https://github.com/egeerardyn/awesome-LaTeX/raw/master/fig/foss.svg)

##### Quality Check Tools

* [paper-preflight (⭐216)](https://github.com/amos689/paper-preflight) ⭐ 219 | 🐛 0 | 🌐 Python | 📅 2026-10-15 - Checks every cited reference of a LaTeX project against Crossref, dblp, arXiv, DataCite, PubMed and OpenAlex records (existence, metadata mismatches, retractions) and reports undefined, duplicate and unused citation keys. Also available as a pre-commit hook, GitHub Action and MCP server. ![windows](https://github.com/egeerardyn/awesome-LaTeX/raw/master/fig/windows.svg) ![linux](https://github.com/egeerardyn/awesome-LaTeX/raw/master/fig/linux.svg) ![mac](https://github.com/egeerardyn/awesome-LaTeX/raw/master/fig/apple.svg) ![foss](https://github.com/egeerardyn/awesome-LaTeX/raw/master/fig/foss.svg)
* [blacktex (⭐159)](https://github.com/texworld/blacktex) ⭐ 159 | 🐛 1 | 📅 2024-01-22 - Command-line tool that replaces commonly occurring LaTeX anti-patterns and cleans up your files. ![windows](https://github.com/egeerardyn/awesome-LaTeX/raw/master/fig/windows.svg) ![linux](https://github.com/egeerardyn/awesome-LaTeX/raw/master/fig/linux.svg) ![mac](https://github.com/egeerardyn/awesome-LaTeX/raw/master/fig/apple.svg) ![foss](https://github.com/egeerardyn/awesome-LaTeX/raw/master/fig/foss.svg)

##### Tools centered around equations

* [IguanaTex (⭐1.5k)](https://github.com/Jonathan-LeRoux/IguanaTex) ⭐ 1,467 | 🐛 6 | 🌐 VBA | 📅 2026-09-06 - PowerPoint add-in that inserts LaTeX equations typeset by your local TeX distribution and keeps the source with each shape, so you can edit and re-typeset it later. ![windows](https://github.com/egeerardyn/awesome-LaTeX/raw/master/fig/windows.svg) ![mac](https://github.com/egeerardyn/awesome-LaTeX/raw/master/fig/apple.svg) ![foss](https://github.com/egeerardyn/awesome-LaTeX/raw/master/fig/foss.svg)
* [LaTeXEqEdit](https://latexeqedit.sourceforge.net/) - LaTeX formula editor for Windows. ![windows](https://github.com/egeerardyn/awesome-LaTeX/raw/master/fig/windows.svg) ![foss](https://github.com/egeerardyn/awesome-LaTeX/raw/master/fig/foss.svg)
* [Mathpix](https://mathpix.com/) - OCR app and API that converts images and PDFs of printed or handwritten equations, tables, and text into LaTeX. Freemium; paid Pro and API.
* [MathPoint](https://tex64.com/mathpoint) - PowerPoint and Word add-in that typesets LaTeX equations, TikZ figures and tables on a server, so no local TeX installation is needed. Freemium.

##### LaTeX-compatible GUI tools

* [TikzEdt](http://www.tikzedt.org) (also: [GitHub repo (⭐10)](https://github.com/hchapman/tikzedt) ⭐ 10 | 🐛 35 | 🌐 C# | 📅 2015-06-08) - WYSIWYG and text-based editor for TikZ pictures. ![foss](https://github.com/egeerardyn/awesome-LaTeX/raw/master/fig/foss.svg)
* [RustFig](https://fabianhassler.github.io/rustfig/) - xfig rebuilt for Windows, macOS, Linux and the browser (no X server): LaTeX text typeset on the canvas, and every fig2dev export built in (TikZ, PSTricks, pict2e, pstex/pdftex, PDF, EPS, SVG). Reads and writes xfig's .fig files. ![windows](https://github.com/egeerardyn/awesome-LaTeX/raw/master/fig/windows.svg) ![linux](https://github.com/egeerardyn/awesome-LaTeX/raw/master/fig/linux.svg) ![mac](https://github.com/egeerardyn/awesome-LaTeX/raw/master/fig/apple.svg) ![foss](https://github.com/egeerardyn/awesome-LaTeX/raw/master/fig/foss.svg)

##### Tables

* [csv2latex](https://freshmeat.sourceforge.net/projects/csv2latex) - Converts CSV files from your favorite programs to LaTeX `tabular`s. ![linux](https://github.com/egeerardyn/awesome-LaTeX/raw/master/fig/linux.svg) ![mac](https://github.com/egeerardyn/awesome-LaTeX/raw/master/fig/apple.svg)

##### Graphics / TikZ

* [TeXample](https://texample.net) - Blog about LaTeX, with a big collection of TikZ figures.
* [pgfplots](https://pgfplots.sourceforge.net) - Truly awesome plotting library on top of and in the style of TikZ/pgf. This library can load in CSV data files, perform some calculations and create beautiful plots.
* [TikZJax](https://tikzjax.com) - Renders TikZ diagrams to SVG in the browser by running TeX via WebAssembly. ![foss](https://github.com/egeerardyn/awesome-LaTeX/raw/master/fig/foss.svg)

##### Templates / TikZ

* [LaTeX templates](https://latextemplates.com) - Collection of templates for papers, posters, resumés, theses, books, presentations, … for LaTeX.

##### Resources / TikZ

* [BibTeX Style Examples](https://kjt.stir.ac.uk/software/latex/showbst.html) - Example output of common BibTeX styles (BST files).

##### Books / TikZ

* [Formatting Information (2020)](https://latex.silmaril.ie/formattinginformation/) - This is the HTML5-based online version of the book *Formatting Information - An introduction to typesetting with LATEX*. It has been continuously updated since the early 2000s.

#### [13. Awesome Gemini Cli](/content/Piebald-AI/awesome-gemini-cli/README.md)

##### Commands & Extensions

* [Wiro AI (⭐1)](https://github.com/wiroai/Wiro-Plugin) ⭐ 1 | 🐛 0 | 🌐 JavaScript | 📅 2026-11-21 - Gemini CLI extension for the hosted Wiro AI MCP server (`https://mcp.wiro.ai/v1`): run 1,000+ image, video, audio and LLM models, build and run workflows, and deploy and message AI agents from Gemini CLI. Ships five Agent Skills, including project media assets (images, icons, short videos, voice-overs and 3D models placed where the framework expects them) and realtime voice. Paid runs bill your Wiro AI wallet and show the price before they start. Install: `gemini extensions install https://github.com/wiroai/Wiro-Plugin`, then `/mcp auth wiro` to sign in.

##### MCP Servers

* [ClickHouse Loom (⭐171)](https://github.com/ClickHouse/loom) ⭐ 186 | 🐛 86 | 🌐 Python | 📅 2026-11-22 - Shared long-term memory, document search and a code index for Gemini CLI through a self-hosted Loom MCP server. Install the release-candidate extension: `gemini extensions install https://github.com/ClickHouse/loom --ref v1.3.0-rc.1`.
* [Brilliant (⭐1)](https://github.com/brilliant-hq/brilliant-mcp) ⭐ 1 | 🐛 0 | 🌐 JavaScript | 📅 2026-11-20 - MCP server that lets Gemini CLI create and edit vector designs on a Brilliant canvas you edit by hand at the same time. Install: `gemini mcp add brilliant -- npx -y @brilliant-hq/mcp`.

#### [14. Free for Dev](/content/ripienaar/free-for-dev/README.md)

##### Source Code Repos

* [Upstream](https://upstream.codes/) - Over 10,000 pushes/sec, the fastest Git host. 100 free repos, public or private, with LFS, merge checks, semantic code search, and AI

##### APIs, Data, and ML

* [Context.dev](https://www.context.dev/) - Web scraping API with 1,000 API credits per month (1,000 standard scrapes) plus 10,000 one-time Logo Link requests, no credit card required.
* [CurrencyBeacon](https://currencybeacon.com/) - Realtime currency data API for fintech apps. The free plan includes 5,000 calls per month.

##### Tools for Teams and Collaboration

* [Fibery](https://fibery.com/) - Connected workspace platform. Free for single users, up to 2 GB disk space.

##### Code Quality

* [DeepSource](https://deepsource.com/) - DeepSource continuously analyzes source code changes, finding and fixing issues categorized under security, performance, anti-patterns, bug-risks, documentation, and style. Native integration with GitHub, GitLab, and Bitbucket.

##### Email

* [turboSMTP](https://serversmtp.com) - SMTP relay service, free for 200 emails per day or 6000 emails per month, free forever, no time limit, no expiry. Web API, SPF/DKIM authentication, suppression management, 1 sending domain, 2 API keys, 2FA, ticket and chat support, GDPR compliant.

##### Forms

* [FormTango](https://formtango.com) - Form backend for static sites and AI-built apps. Free plan: 100 submissions a month, unlimited forms, spam filtering and email notifications. Endpoints can be created from the API with no account.
* [Kwes.io](https://kwesforms.com/) - Feature rich form endpoint. Works great with static sites. The free plan includes up to 1 website with up to 50 monthly submissions.

##### BaaS

* [IFTTT](https://ifttt.ai/) - Automate your favorite apps and devices. Free 2 Applets

##### Managed Data Services

* [CrateDB](https://cratedb.com/) - Distributed Open Source SQL database for real-time analytics. [Free Tier CRFREE](https://crate.io/lp-crfree): One-node with 2 CPUs, 2 GiB of memory, 8 GiB of storage. One cluster per organization, no payment method needed.
